```text
Important results from the Ign-F1 sync Worker:

Key findings:
1. **Rankings basically unchanged** — only r2a/r2b swap (within noise)
2. **RSFT best (s43) vs SFT baseline**: 5.77pp → 5.49pp gap (shrinks but still significant)

4. **GRPO bf16 vs RSFT CED s43**: -1.34pp → -0.26pp — GRPO almost catches up to RSFT. This is interesting.


5. **flat-NLI s43** is missing metrics_v2.json and needs the Ign-F1 fix applied, while s44 and s45 are still running on different GPUs.
6. There's something odd with GRPO bf16's Ign-F1 being 0.10pp higher than regular F1, which shouldn't happen since filtering should only decrease the score — unless the filtered training triples happen to be ones GRPO struggles with.

Actually, thinking through this more carefully: Ign-F1 removes triples that appear in training data from both predictions and gold labels. If some of those removed triples were false positives, precision could actually improve, making Ign-F1 > F1 possible in edge cases, though it's rare. The important thing is that none of these metric shifts change the ranking of claims. I need to update the evidence table in paper_framing.md with the corrected Ign-F1 values and report this to the Director, then spawn a quick Worker to apply the metrics_v2.json fix to s43.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: POST-MVP + DREAM v2 完成（D047 分析）\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-16\\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5 → best 提升但 median 下降，未达标。关键路径：plan_011 bf16 GRPO eval + structural fixes → re-dream v3\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。\\n\\n我们系统性地探索了 online RL (GRPO) 、offline preference optimization (DPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 三条技术路线。关键发现：**RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关**——优化强度越大，evidence 质量提升的同时抽取能力退化越严重。QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（rel_f1<0.02），bf16 GRPO 从 0.4549 退化至 ~0.434（mild, -2pp），DPO 呈 gradual 退化（1ep→3ep rel_f1 从 0.4034→0.3429），RSFT 无 decoupling。\\n\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 作为 RSFT 的 scoring function 之一进行测试，其相对于 flat-NLI 的优势需要通过充分的统计检验验证。\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\n3. **Cost of faithfulness**: evidence generation 代价 ~8pp rel_f1（no-evidence 0.4893 vs evidence-SFT 0.4106）——这是 faithfulness 的真实成本\\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline preference (DPO) vs offline RL (RSFT)\\n5. **Key finding**: evidence-extraction decoupling — RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关，形成连续谱系（GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none）\\n6. **QLoRA amplification**: QLoRA 量化将 mild decoupling 放大至 catastrophic forgetting——decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度，QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier\\n7. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 是唯一有效路线——提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\\n8. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价（0.4864 vs 0.4893），同时保留高质量 evidence（evi_f1=0.8188）\\n9. **Diagnostic tool**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling（与 Claim 2 呼应），per-relation 级揭示 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 难度差异（D070 ✅）\\n\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068/D069/D070)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\n- FREIGE 是首个在 DocRED 上同时输出 (head, relation, tail, [evidence]) 的 LLM 方法\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: no-evidence SFT rel_f1=0.4893 vs evidence-SFT 0.4106，代价 ~8pp（plan_014 ✅）\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 配置下均超过 SFT baseline（rel_f1 +3.9~6.0pp）\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, step_70=0.4341）— 退化可控但持续（D065/D066）\\n  - DPO bf16 1ep→3ep: gradual（rel_f1 0.4034→0.3429, evi_f1 0.8088→↑）— 随训练渐进退化（D067/D068）\\n  - RSFT: none — 无 decoupling，唯一有效路线\\n- **QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\"\\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都优于 random/no scoring\\n- CED vs flat-NLI 的差异需统计验证（Dream 报告 p=0.08/0.38/0.12 不显著）\\n- **Critical**: 如果 CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI → CED 降级为\\\"one possible scoring function\\\"\\n- **行动**: 5+ seeds for significance testing\\n\\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070 重定位 + per-relation 验证 ✅）\\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.965（plan_003 ✅）——本质是 decoupling 的度量\\n  - EDCR 识别病理模型（GRPO QLoRA: EDCR≈1.0）\\n- **Layer 2 — per-relation-type**: EDCR 提供 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 诊断信息 ✅\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 relation 级是难度诊断工具（纵向定位薄弱 relation）——两层叙事互补\\n- **仍需**: 100 例人工标注验证（RC6）\\n\\n## MVP Evidence Summary\\n\\n| Config | rel_f1 | Δ rel_f1 | evi_f1 (conditional) | evi_f1 (joint, TBD) | EDCR |\\n|--------|--------|----------|---------------------|---------------------|------|\\n| **SFT no-evidence** | **0.4893** | **+8.36pp** | **—** | **—** | **—** |\\n| SFT baseline | 0.4057 | — | 0.7979 | 0.3117 | 0.7118 |\\n| **CED reranker** | **0.4864** | **+8.07pp** | **0.8188** | — | **0.6156** |\\n| RSFT r1 (3ep, s42) | 0.4449 | +3.92pp | 0.8187 | 0.3468 | 0.6565 |\\n| RSFT r1 (s44) | 0.4655 | +5.98pp | 0.8087 | 0.3713 | 0.6350 |\\n| RSFT r2a (1ep) | 0.4494 | +4.37pp | 0.8108 | 0.3509 | 0.6641 |\\n| RSFT r2b (1ep, conservative) | 0.4493 | +4.36pp | 0.8128 | 0.3490 | 0.6609 |\\n| DPO bf16 1ep | 0.4034 | -0.23pp | 0.8088 | — | 0.7139 |\\n| DPO bf16 3ep | 0.3429 | -6.28pp | — | — | — |\\n| GRPO bf16 G=8 (best step) | ~0.434 | +2.83pp | 0.82 | — | — |\\n| GRPO bf16 G=8 (baseline ckpt-100) | 0.4549 | — | — | — | — |\\n| GRPO QLoRA (lr=5e-6, ckpt-100) | 0.0138 | -39.2pp | 0.8500 | — | 0.9900 |\\n\\n注：evi_f1 (conditional) 仅计算 TP triples 的 evidence，与 DREEAM joint protocol 不可比（D041）。GRPO bf16 baseline ckpt-100 是训练前起点，非 GRPO 训练结果。\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns (updated from Dream, D068/D069)\\n\\n### RC1: \\\"Online RL\\\" 标题过度声称（Dream 三档一致 Critical）\\n- ~~标题和 claim 收窄到 \\\"GRPO fails for DocRE\\\"~~\\n- **D068/D069 修正**: 重新定位为 \\\"RL optimization pressure correlates with decoupling severity\\\"——不是 GRPO 特有问题，而是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度\\n- DPO 数据支撑 decoupling spectrum（gradual decay confirms the pattern）\\n\\n### RC2: GRPO G=4 太小 + LoRA confound（Dream 三档一致 Major）\\n- GRPO G=8 bf16 实验 ✅ — 确认 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier（D065/D066）\\n- LoRA confound 已解决：bf16 LoRA vs QLoRA 直接对比\\n\\n### RC3: CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI（Dream 三档一致 Major）\\n- 5+ seeds 统计检验；如不显著 → CED 降级\\n\\n### RC4: RSFT 不新颖\\n- 不声称 RSFT 是贡献；贡献在于任务定义 + decoupling 发现 + EDCR\\n\\n### RC5: 评测可比性（D041）\\n- DREEAM-protocol Evi-F1；conditional Evi-F1 作为辅助指标\\n\\n### RC6: EDCR 缺人工验证\\n- 100 例小规模标注\\n\\n### RC7: Missing DPO/PPO（Dream 两档 Major）\\n- DPO experiment ✅ — 结果确认 decoupling spectrum（D067/D068）\\n\\n### RC8: Dev-test degradation\\n- 观察项，等 test set 数据\\n\\n### RC9: 连接 reward overoptimization 文献\\n- 显式引用 Gao et al. 2023\\n\\n### RC10: Post-hoc evidence retrieval baseline\\n- SFT + BM25 evidence retrieval\\n\\n## Key Baselines (12+)\\n\\n1. **SFT-only**: Qwen3-4B + LoRA SFT（with evidence）\\n1b. **SFT no-evidence**: Qwen3-4B + LoRA SFT（无 evidence，upper bound ablation）\\n2. **RSFT flat-NLI**: flat-NLI scored RSFT（核心 ablation）\\n3. **RSFT CED**: CED scored RSFT（主方法）\\n4. **CED-as-reranker**: 推理时 CED 重排（无训练）\\n5. **DPO+CED**: offline preference learning — gradual decoupling（D067/D068）\\n6. **GRPO+CED (QLoRA, G=4)**: catastrophic decoupling baseline\\n7. **GRPO+CED (bf16, G=8)**: mild decoupling — QLoRA confound 排除（D065/D066）\\n8. **Post-hoc retrieval**: SFT + BM25 evidence（NEW）\\n9. **DREEAM**: encoder evidence SOTA\\n10. **SAIS**: encoder evidence baseline\\n11. **DocKS-RAG**: extraction SOTA（无 evidence）\\n12. **LMRC**: generative LLM SOTA（无 evidence）\\n\\n## Publishability Threshold (revised after Dream, D068/D069)\\n\\n- **必要条件**: (1) RSFT 在 test set 上 rel_f1 显著 > SFT (2) DREEAM-protocol Evi-F1 可报告 (3) Decoupling spectrum 有完整数据支撑（GRPO QLoRA/bf16 + DPO + RSFT）\\n- **充分条件**: 上述 + CED 显著 > flat-NLI + EDCR 人工验证\\n- **Failure pivot**: decoupling spectrum 不成立（bf16 GRPO 也 catastrophic）→ scope 大幅缩减。**已排除**: bf16 GRPO 确认 mild（D065/D066）\\n\\n## Research Phase Priorities (revised after Dream v1)\\n\\n| Priority | 实验 | Claim | GPU-h FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: POST-MVP + DREAM v2 完成（D047 分析）\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-16\\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5 → best 提升但 median 下降，未达标。关键路径：plan_011 bf16 GRPO eval + structural fixes → re-dream v3\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。\\n\\n我们系统性地探索了 online RL (GRPO) 、offline preference optimization (DPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 三条技术路线。关键发现：**RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关**——优化强度越大，evidence 质量提升的同时抽取能力退化越严重。QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（rel_f1<0.02），bf16 GRPO 从 0.4549 退化至 ~0.434（mild, -2pp），DPO 呈 gradual 退化（1ep→3ep rel_f1 从 0.4034→0.3429），RSFT 无 decoupling。\\n\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 作为 RSFT 的 scoring function 之一进行测试，其相对于 flat-NLI 的优势需要通过充分的统计检验验证。\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\n3. **Cost of faithfulness**: evidence generation 代价 ~8pp rel_f1（no-evidence 0.4893 vs evidence-SFT 0.4106）——这是 faithfulness 的真实成本\\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline preference (DPO) vs offline RL (RSFT)\\n5. **Key finding**: evidence-extraction decoupling — RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关，形成连续谱系（GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none）\\n6. **QLoRA amplification**: QLoRA 量化将 mild decoupling 放大至 catastrophic forgetting——decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度，QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier\\n7. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 是唯一有效路线——提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\\n8. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价（0.4864 vs 0.4893），同时保留高质量 evidence（evi_f1=0.8188）\\n9. **Diagnostic tool**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling（与 Claim 2 呼应），per-relation 级揭示 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 难度差异（D070 ✅）\\n\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068/D069/D070)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\n- FREIGE 是首个在 DocRED 上同时输出 (head, relation, tail, [evidence]) 的 LLM 方法\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: no-evidence SFT rel_f1=0.4893 vs evidence-SFT 0.4106，代价 ~8pp（plan_014 ✅）\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 配置下均超过 SFT baseline（rel_f1 +3.9~6.0pp）\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, step_70=0.4341）— 退化可控但持续（D065/D066）\\n  - DPO bf16 1ep→3ep: gradual（rel_f1 0.4034→0.3429, evi_f1 0.8088→↑）— 随训练渐进退化（D067/D068）\\n  - RSFT: none — 无 decoupling，唯一有效路线\\n- **QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\"\\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都优于 random/no scoring\\n- CED vs flat-NLI 的差异需统计验证（Dream 报告 p=0.08/0.38/0.12 不显著）\\n- **Critical**: 如果 CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI → CED 降级为\\\"one possible scoring function\\\"\\n- **行动**: 5+ seeds for significance testing\\n\\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070 重定位 + per-relation 验证 ✅）\\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.965（plan_003 ✅）——本质是 decoupling 的度量\\n  - EDCR 识别病理模型（GRPO QLoRA: EDCR≈1.0）\\n- **Layer 2 — per-relation-type**: EDCR 提供 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 诊断信息 ✅\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 relation 级是难度诊断工具（纵向定位薄弱 relation）——两层叙事互补\\n- **仍需**: 100 例人工标注验证（RC6）\\n\\n## MVP Evidence {
```
```json
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "file": "paper_framing.md",
  "content": "# Paper Framing — FREIGE\n\n> **状态**: POST-MVP + DREAM v2 完成（D047 分析）\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-16\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5 → best 提升但 median 下降，未达标。关键路径：plan_011 bf16 GRPO eval + structural fixes → re-dream v3\n\n## Target Venue\n\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\n\n## Core Insight\n\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。\n\n我们系统性地探索了 online RL (GRPO) 、offline preference optimization (DPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 三条技术路线。关键发现：**RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关**——优化强度越大，evidence 质量提升的同时抽取能力退化越严重。QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（rel_f1<0.02），bf16 GRPO 从 0.4549 退化至 ~0.434（mild, -2pp），DPO 呈 gradual 退化（1ep→3ep rel_f1 从 0.4034→0.3429），RSFT 无 decoupling。\n\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 作为 RSFT 的 scoring function 之一进行测试，其相对于 flat-NLI 的优势需要通过充分的统计检验验证。\n\n## Story Arc\n\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\n3. **Cost of faithfulness**: evidence generation 代价 ~7pp Ign-F1（no-evidence 47.36 vs evidence-SFT 40.36）——这是 faithfulness 的真实成本\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline preference (DPO) vs offline RL (RSFT)\n5. **Key finding**: evidence-extraction decoupling — RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关，形成连续谱系（GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none）\n6. **QLoRA amplification**: QLoRA 量化将 mild decoupling 放大至 catastrophic forgetting——decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度，QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier\n7. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 是唯一有效路线——提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\n8. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价，同时保留高质量 evidence（evi_f1=0.8188）\n9. **Diagnostic tool**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling（与 Claim 2 呼应），per-relation 级揭示 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 难度差异（D070 ✅）\n\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068/D069/D070/D071/D072)\n\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\n- FREIGE 是首个在 DocRED 上同时输出 (head, relation, tail, [evidence]) 的 LLM 方法\n- **Evidence generation tax**: no-evidence SFT Ign-F1=47.36 vs evidence-SFT 40.36，代价 ~7pp（plan_014 ✅）\n- RSFT 在 4/4 配置下均超过 SFT baseline（Ign-F1 +3.3~5.5pp）\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\n\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, step_70=0.4341）— 退化可控但持续（D065/D066）\n  - DPO bf16 1ep→3ep: gradual（Ign-F1 38.88→TBD, rel_f1 0.4034→0.3429）— 随训练渐进退化（D067/D068）\n  - RSFT: none — 无 decoupling，唯一有效路线\n- **QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\"genuinely novel and striking\"\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\n\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都优于 random/no scoring\n- CED vs flat-NLI 的差异需统计验证（Dream 报告 p=0.08/0.38/0.12 不显著）\n- **Critical**: 如果 CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI → CED 降级为\"one possible scoring function\"\n- **行动**: 5+ seeds for significance testing\n\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070/D072）\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.
```

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user (Director) is managing an ML research project "faith-evidence-ie" targeting EMNLP 2026 submission. The project studies LLM-based evidence-grounded Document-level Relation Extraction (FREIGE). This session continued from a compacted previous conversation. The Director's instructions across this session:
   
   - Complete pending Claim 2 reframing in paper_framing.md (D068/D069): from "GRPO fails" to "RL optimization pressure correlates with decoupling severity"
   - Process Reviewer feedback on plan_003 EDCR diagnostic: (1) fix stale .tex data, (2) per-relation EDCR analysis for Claim 4 (D070), (3) supplement missing evi_joint_f1
   - Accept Claim 4 dual-layer positioning (D072): method-level EDCR as decoupling metric + per-relation EDCR as independent diagnostic tool
   - Correct GRPO bf16 data: 0.4549 is ckpt-100 (pre-training baseline), actual results ~0.434
   - Ign-F1 bug: fix evaluator.py, main table reports Ign-F1, appendix reports F1 (D071)
   - Sync all Ign-F1 corrected data, check ranking changes
   - CED s43/s45 seed training: highest priority for paired t-test (s43 on cuda:1 immediately, s45 queued)
   - Fix failed rsft_ced_s43_train (wrong cwd + missing params), resubmit with Director's corrected command template
   - Maintain Worker pool ≥3 at all times

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **FREIGE**: LLM-based evidence-grounded DocRE system
   - **CED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination)**: NLI-based scoring for evidence quality
   - **RSFT (Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning)**: Training with CED/NLI-filtered positive examples
   - **EDCR (Evidence-Decoupling Ratio)**: Metric measuring gap between rel_f1 and evi_f1; dual-layer diagnostic (method-level + per-relation)
   - **Evidence-extraction decoupling spectrum**: GRPO QLoRA (catastrophic, rel_f1=0.014) > GRPO bf16 (mild, 0.4549→0.434, -2pp) > DPO bf16 (gradual, 0.4034→0.3429) > RSFT (none)
   - **Ign-F1**: DocRED convention metric excluding training triples. Bug: evaluator.py `_triple_key` included doc_id, but train/dev have 0 overlapping documents → Ign filter was no-op
   - **Per-relation EDCR**: ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = -0.064 at per-relation level proves EDCR provides independent diagnostic information
   - **Joint vs Conditional Evi-F1**: Joint counts FN relations as 0 evidence score; conditional only evaluates TP triples' evidence
   - **State machine**: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE (states only go forward)
   - **submit_on_allocated**: Requires top-level server/gpu/account fields in registry + bash -c wrapper for tmux
   - **metrics_v2.json**: Corrected Ign-F1 values stored alongside original metrics.json

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **paper_framing.md** (`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/paper_framing.md`)
     - Central paper strategy document. Updated 4 times this session:
     - (1) Claim 2 reframed from "GRPO fails" to decoupling spectrum (D068/D069)
     - (2) GRPO bf16 data corrected: 0.4549 → ~0.434 (-2pp), added step-level data

     - (4) Evidence table converted to Ign-F1 primary metric (D071), added F1/joint evi_f1 columns
     - Final evidence table uses Ign-F1 as primary metric with corrected values (SFT baseline 40.36, RSFT CED s43 best 45.85, etc.)

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01UV3fSy5iBhmqcdRu9X4whM`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

```text
2. **CED s45 training param verification** — I'm checking whether the batch size configurations are actually equivalent. s43 uses batch 1 with gradient accumulation of 16 for an effective batch of 16, while s45 uses per_device_batch_size 4 with gradient accumulation steps of 4, which also gives an effective batch of 16. The micro-batch sizes differ but the overall training dynamics should be comparable.
3. **Update report.md** — I can do this myself since it's a local file operation
4. **Prepare seed comparison analysis** — when evals complete

But wait, for a proper paired t-test I need identical configs except for the seed, and I'm not even certain the rsft_trainer uses those exact parameter names. The directory_check worker submitted s45 with those params, but s43 used different names that worked fine. I should spawn a worker to SSH in and verify what arguments the trainer actually accepts, check s45's running config from its logs, and potentially restart s45 if the configurations don't match. Reviewer 审查发现两个严重问题：
```

### 1. [CRITICAL] rsft_ced_s45_train_r2 数据路径错误
当前 spec 用的是 `rsft_filtered_topk3_ced_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl` (7942 samples)，这是 plan_008 的数据。CED s42/s44 用的是 `rsft_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl` (2718 samples)。paired t-test 要求所有 seed 用相同数据，否则统计结论无效。**立即修正 s45 的 data_path**。

### 2. [WARNING] rsft_ced_s43_train_r2 超参可能不对
我之前给的模板是 flat-NLI 配置（batch_size=1, grad_accum=16），但 CED s42/s44 原始配置是 batch_size=4, grad_accum=4，且有 --weighted_sampling，无 --bf16。

**立即检查**：rsft_ced_s43_train_r2 当前是否在跑？用了什么超参？
- 如果用了 flat-NLI 超参（batch 1/grad_accum 16）→ kill 并用下面的正确模板重提交
- 如果已经用了 CED 超参 → 继续跑

**CED 正确命令模板（s43 和 s45 通用，只改 seed/output_dir/wandb_run_name）**：
```
cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.rsft_trainer \
  --base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B \
  --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output \
  --rsft_data_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl \
  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_ced_s43 \
  --learning_rate 2e-5 --num_epochs 3 \
  --per_device_batch_size 4 --gradient_accumulation_steps 4 \
  --lora_rank 64 --lora_alpha 128 \
  --weighted_sampling \
  --eval_data_dir /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred --eval_max_docs 50 \
  --wandb_run_name rsft_ced_s43 --seed 43
```

CED 补种是 paired t-test 阻塞项，优先级最高。

Both need SSH and registry access to handle the kill-and-resubmit workflow. I need to verify the actual configurations by spawning workers to SSH into the systems and check the logs. For s43, the batch size is definitely wrong—it's using flat-NLI settings instead of CED's required per-device batch size and gradient accumulation steps, so it needs to be killed and resubmitted with the correct parameters. For s45, I need to check the actual training log to see if the data path issue the Director flagged is real or a false alarm.

````text
1. Update old registry entries to DONE/killed

3. Submit with Director's correct command template

Let me update registry first, then submit.

The correct command template from Director:
```
cd /root/autodl-tmp && python -m freige.training.rsft_trainer \
  --base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B \
  --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output \
  --rsft_data_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered/rsft_train.jsonl \
  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_output_ced_s{SEED} \
  --learning_rate 2e-5 --num_epochs 3 \
  --per_device_batch_size 4 --gradient_accumulation_steps 4 \
  --lora_rank 64 --lora_alpha 128 \
  --weighted_sampling \
  --eval_data_dir /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred --eval_max_docs 50 \
  --wandb_run_name
```

I'm setting up the training command with the seed parameter, and I need to wrap it in a bash shell with conda activation since the submit_training_job function requires sourcing conda. Looking at previous successful runs, they used the full conda initialization path, but the template doesn't include that—I should check if python is already available in the PATH on this server or if I need to explicitly activate the conda environment.

For the two training runs, I'm configuring s43 with cuda:1 and seed 43, and s45 with cuda:2 and seed 45, each with their own output directories and wandb run names. I need to be careful about the output directory for s43 since it might have partial checkpoints from a previous run, so the new training will overwrite those. I need to add cleanup commands to remove the partial output directories before restarting training for s43 and s45, then update the registry to reflect the killed experiments.
````
